# Bundled CUA Windows dependency notices

This distribution includes CUA Driver 0.37.0 and the dependencies inventoried
in `runtime-sbom.json`. License choices and copyright notices are retained below.
The inventory conservatively includes normal and build dependencies of the main
executable, UIA helper and SDK DLL for Windows x64, excluding development edges.
It may include crates not linked into an individual binary.

Source form for every registry package is available at the exact-version source
URL in `runtime-sbom.json` (the publicly downloadable crates.io source archive).
This includes the Mozilla Public License 2.0 UniFFI crates. Their covered source
is available under MPL-2.0 at those URLs; no modifications to those crates are
made by Collie. CUA's own MIT license and derivative notices are in
`LICENSE-CUA.md` and `CUA-THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`. Collie's license does not
replace any bundled component's license. Source availability imposes no charge.

### SHA-256 0a0e814a210338c57b9d65eb516546045c0ca8caa308bf29aa3736236b08346b

Applies to:

- rquickjs-sys 0.14.0: vendor/wasi-libc/NOTICE.md

````text
# Vendored wasi-libc

`include/` and `lib/libc.a` in this directory are vendored from the
wasi-sdk-24 release (`wasi-sysroot-24.0.tar.gz`,
sha256 `35172f7d2799485b15a46b1d87f50a585d915ec662080f005d99153a50888f08`)
by `scripts/vendor-wasm-libc.sh`. Do not hand-edit them; re-run the script.

- `lib/libc.a`: the wasm32-wasi build of wasi-libc, unmodified.
- `include/`: the subset of wasi-libc's public headers that quickjs's C
  sources need to compile against `libc.a` for `wasm32-unknown-unknown`.
  `wasi/api.h` carries one commented-out `#error` (see the patch step in
  `vendor-wasm-libc.sh`) so it can be included outside an actual wasi
  target; the rest are otherwise unmodified aside from the provenance
  comment the script prepends to each file.

wasi-libc is licensed MIT / Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception / Apache-2.0
(https://github.com/WebAssembly/wasi-libc/blob/main/LICENSE); wasi-sdk,
which builds and publishes this compiled artifact, is Apache-2.0 WITH
LLVM-exception (https://github.com/WebAssembly/wasi-sdk/blob/main/LICENSE).
Both are permissive with no copyleft terms; only `libc.a`'s object code and
the headers above are vendored here, not wasi-sdk's clang/LLVM toolchain.

To update: bump `WASI_SDK_VERSION_MAJOR`/`MINOR` and `WASI_SYSROOT_SHA256`
at the top of `vendor-wasm-libc.sh` (the checksum is published on the
release page, https://github.com/WebAssembly/wasi-sdk/releases/tag/wasi-sdk-<major>),
then re-run `sh scripts/vendor-wasm-libc.sh` from the repo root.
````

### SHA-256 8ee5d08d14d9226c4baf34587a0352dc5657dcfad9ebeef18e001e2a72eb00b0

Applies to:

- cua-telemetry 0.1.0: src/notice.rs

````text
//! The first-run notice. Nothing is sent from a machine until it has been
//! shown there once (on stderr by the CLI and SDKs, in the onboarding by
//! the Spaces app).

/// The docs page.
pub const DOCS_URL: &str = "https://cua.ai/docs/cua-sdk/concepts/telemetry";

/// Marker file under `$CUA_HOME/telemetry` once the notice was shown.
pub const MARKER: &str = "notice_shown";

/// The notice, as printed.
pub const TEXT: &str = "\
Cua collects usage data to help improve it: which commands and features you use,
how long things take and what kind of errors happen. If you sign in, it's linked
to your Cua account through a random ID. Never your files, file paths, names,
what you type, your screen or anything from your Keyvault. Nothing has been sent yet.
  Turn it off:  cua telemetry off   (or DO_NOT_TRACK=1, or CUA_TELEMETRY=0)
  See what is sent:  cua telemetry show-last
  Details: https://cua.ai/docs/cua-sdk/concepts/telemetry";
````

### SHA-256 b3d734001a94efff3579978d953391aa7115f877657d25eb54037a43875d078a

Applies to:

- ring 0.17.14: LICENSE

````text
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

See LICENSE-BoringSSL for code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the
Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

See src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-APACHE and src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-MIT
for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
````
